/****************************************************/
/* Test binary search with all storage combinations */
/****************************************************/

/*
Storage combinations:

g = grapheme
c = codepoint
e = error
_ = separator between start and end storage

gce_gce
-------
000_000     _
000_001     _e
000_010     _c
000_011     _ce
000_100     _g
000_101     _ge
000_110     _gc
000_111     _gce

<cut>

cge_cge
-------
111_000     gce_
111_001     gce_e
111_010     gce_c
111_011     gce_ce
111_100     gce_g
111_101     gce_ge
111_110     gce_gc
111_111     gce_gce
*/




/* ************************************************* */
/* ""                                                */
/* (ASCII, 0 grapheme, 0 codepoint, 0 byte, 0 error) */
/* ************************************************* */

-- 1.1.1.1
-- "_" Non-incremental forward binary search codepoints
indexer = .RexxUnicodeStringIndexer~new("", 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0)
indexer~limits= -- [ 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0]
indexer~startGraphemeIndexes= -- []
indexer~startCodepointIndexes= -- []
indexer~startErrorMessages= -- []
indexer~endGraphemeIndexes= -- []
indexer~endCodepointIndexes= -- []
indexer~endErrorMessages= -- []
do i = 1 to indexer~string~length; indexer~codepointIndexC(i); end
indexer~startGraphemeIndexes= -- []
indexer~startCodepointIndexes= -- []
indexer~startErrorMessages= -- []
indexer~endGraphemeIndexes= -- []
indexer~endCodepointIndexes= -- []
indexer~endErrorMessages= -- []
